if _G.FEMLOADER_CLEANUP then pcall(_G.FEMLOADER_CLEANUP) end
local GAMES = {
    { id = "delta", name = "Project Delta",
      builds = {
        { id = "paid",    name = "Buyer",   script = "64712896631643315832" },
        { id = "tester",  name = "Tester",  script = "99579693848200936491",
          note = "tester" },
        { id = "private", name = "Private", script = "98760938609412333774",
          note = "developer" },
      },
      places = { 7336302630 }, group = 3765739,
      place = 7336302630, banner = "", url = "", tint = Color3.fromRGB(150, 100, 255) },
    { id = "fallen", name = "Fallen",
      builds = {
        { id = "paid",    name = "Paid",    script = "76086507665800834919" },
        { id = "private", name = "Private", script = "76670689068126203772",
          note = "developer" },
      },
      places = { 10228136016 }, group = 1154360,
      unreleased = true,
      place = 10228136016, banner = "", url = "", tint = Color3.fromRGB(255,  90,  90) },
    { id = "universal", name = "Universal",
      builds = {
        { id = "main",   name = "Main",   script = "" },
        { id = "tester", name = "Tester", script = "" },
      },
      fallback = true,
      unreleased = true,
      places = {},
      place = nil, banner = "", url = "", tint = Color3.fromRGB( 90, 200, 255) },
}
local HERE  = game.PlaceId
local GROUP = (game.CreatorType == Enum.CreatorType.Group) and game.CreatorId or nil
local HOME = (function()
    for _, e in ipairs(GAMES) do
        for _, id in ipairs(e.places or {}) do
            if id == HERE then return e.id end
        end
    end
    if GROUP then
        for _, e in ipairs(GAMES) do
            if e.group == GROUP then return e.id end
        end
    end
    for _, e in ipairs(GAMES) do
        if e.fallback then return e.id end
    end
end)()
local function isHere(entry) return entry ~= nil and entry.id == HOME end
local function homeEntry()
    for _, e in ipairs(GAMES) do
        if e.id == HOME then return e end
    end
end
local FALLEN_BYPASS_URL =
    "https://femboy-hook.com/fallen.bypass.standalone.lua"
local function fallenBypass()
    local pre = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
    if pre.femhook_bypass_job == tostring(game.JobId)
        and not pre.femhook_bypass_failed
        and (pre.femhook_bypass_loaded or pre.femhook_bypass_ran) then
        return true
    end
    if FALLEN_BYPASS_URL == "" then return false, "no bypass url set" end
    local ok, src = pcall(game.HttpGet, game, FALLEN_BYPASS_URL)
    if not ok or type(src) ~= "string" or src == "" then
        return false, "bypass unreachable"
    end
    local fn, err = loadstring(src)
    if not fn then return false, "bypass failed to compile: " .. tostring(err) end
    local ranOk, runErr = pcall(fn)
    if not ranOk then return false, "bypass errored: " .. tostring(runErr) end
    local genv = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
    if genv.femhook_bypass_failed then return false, "bypass reported failure" end
    if genv.femhook_bypass_loaded or genv.femhook_bypass_ran then return true end
    return false, "bypass did not latch"
end
for _, e in ipairs(GAMES) do
    if e.id == "fallen" then e.prelaunch = fallenBypass end
end
local BOOT_BYPASS = { attempted = false }
do
    local home = homeEntry()
    if home and home.prelaunch then
        BOOT_BYPASS.attempted = true
        BOOT_BYPASS.ok, BOOT_BYPASS.err = home.prelaunch()
        if BOOT_BYPASS.ok then task.wait(1.5) end
    end
end
